# 7.3.1 Wire compatibility results

**1614** DDS exchanges · **1519** passed · **95** failed · **0** not run · **1** known peer exception(s) · **0** case(s) not implemented · **1** case(s) not applicable

Each cell is a writer → reader exchange. A `"` repeats the IDL from the previous row. Observed errors are quoted from sanitized logs.

| Legend | Meaning |
| :----: | :------ |
|   ✅   | Exchange passed |
| 🟧<sup>†</sup> | Passed with an adaptation |
|   ⛔   | Failed |
|   ❔   | Not run |
|   ⚠️ Exception   | Known peer generation or compile issue; see the message in Problem / defect |
| N/A | Peer or pairing is outside this case's configured scope |
|   ⚫   | Not implemented |

Failure details show the observed symptom. Adaptation notes link to the applicable workaround IDs (`W001`–`W018`).

[Workaround guide](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md) explains the default paths and applied code changes.

## Not applicable

Unavailable features are N/A and excluded from exchange counts.

| Case | Reason |
| :--- | :----- |
| `09-must-understand` | The `@must_understand` feature was introduced in RTI 7.7.0 and is rejected by rtiddsgen 4.3.1. Hash-ID coverage is independent in C040 and applies to both LTS versions. |

## Case-specific generation and peer scope

- **04-unbounded-string-aliases**: RTI C++ generation adds `-unboundedSupport`.  [Workaround guide](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md)
- **05-array-of-sequences-idl**: RTI C++ uses the default generation flags. C149 with SequenceArrayMapping=Idl. The duplicate IDL has the same declarations as C018; live DDS results apply only to the recorded fixtures and exact runtime version. [Workaround guide](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md)
  Wireloom configuration: `{"idl":"idl/features/05-array-of-sequences-idl.idl","sequenceArrayMapping":"Idl"}`. Input and generated-source SHA-256 hashes are in the JSON evidence.
  Known exception for **RTI C#**: The direct-form RTI C# binding flattens arrays of sequences and describes a different type.
- **05-unbounded-sequence-aliases**: RTI C++ generation adds `-unboundedSupport`.  [Workaround guide](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md)
- **06-aggregates**: RTI C++ generation adds `-unboundedSupport`. Recursive-structure support is experimental in RTI C++ and requires -unboundedSupport. Direct self-recursive sequence members are exercised by a focused fixture in the standard wire matrix. [Workaround W011](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md#w011), [Workaround W012](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md#w012)
- **07-union-octet**: RTI C++ uses the default generation flags.  [Workaround guide](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md)
- **07-union-wchar-label**: RTI C++ uses the default generation flags. RTI C# is excluded before peer build. Wireloom C# and RTI C++ remain in the matrix. [Workaround W013](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md#w013)
  Known exception for **RTI C#**: RTI rejects the wchar union discriminator during source generation; no RTI C# peer can be built.
- **09-optional-recursive-array**: RTI C++ generation adds `-unboundedSupport`. Recursive-structure support is experimental in RTI C++ and requires -unboundedSupport; this case also exercises optional recursive arrays. [Workaround W007](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md#w007), [Workaround W008](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md#w008), [Workaround W009](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md#w009), [Workaround W010](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md#w010)
- **10-resolve-name-wstring-aliases**: RTI C++ uses the default generation flags.  [Workaround W015](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md#w015)
  Adjustment for **RTI C#**: Uses the checked-in adjusted copy; qualifies two Implementation unmanaged alias references with CorpusResolveNameWstringAliases. Raw captured oracle is preserved unchanged.
  Adjustment for **RTI C++**: The current runner applies a guarded generated TypeCode reference correction to the scratch source; the raw C++ oracle is not retained for this case.
  RTI C# source: checked-in adjusted oracle at `rtiddsgen/csharp/wire-adjusted/10-resolve-name-wstring-aliases`.
- **11-include-search**: RTI C++ uses the default generation flags. The RTI wire oracle bundles the separately captured common.idl output required by the generated include references; the root oracle capture is unchanged. [Workaround guide](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md)
- **11-preprocessing**: RTI C++ uses the default generation flags. The RTI wire oracle bundles the separately captured common.idl output required by the generated include references; the root oracle capture is unchanged. [Workaround guide](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md)
- **14-typed-defaults**: RTI C++ uses the default generation flags.  [Workaround guide](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md)
- **14-unbounded-receive-lifecycle**: RTI C++ generation adds `-unboundedSupport`.  [Workaround guide](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md)
- **14-unbounded-support**: RTI C++ generation adds `-unboundedSupport`.  [Workaround guide](https://github.com/eNeRGy164/wireloom/blob/7ab7834a66358915993e09cba308e1c66183f70d/tests/Wireloom.Dds.Generator.WireCompatibility/WORKAROUNDS.md)
